                                                              Chairman’s Statement

統一企業中國控股有限公司（「本公司」）及其                    Uni-President China Holdings Ltd. (the “Company”) and its
附屬公司（統稱「本集團」或「我們」）秉持「誠                   subsidiaries (together as the “Group”, “our Group”, “we”, “our” or
實勤道、創新求進」的經營理念，堅持「價值營                    “us”) adhere to the corporate vision of “Honesty and Diligence;
銷」，聚 焦 經 營，積 極 構 建 品 牌 價 值，走 穩 健         Innovation and Excellence （誠實勤道、創新求進）” and uphold
經營之道，以「健康操作」為指導原則，致力於                    the principle of “Value Marketing （價值營銷）”. With a focus on
成為一個具備「品格」   、「品牌」與「品味」的三品               operation, we are always vigorous in creating brand value, pursuing
企業。                                      steady operation and aligning to “Healthy Practice （健康操作）”,
                                         with an aim to evolve into an enterprise boasting “Integrity（品格）”,
                                         “Brand（品牌）” and “Taste（品味）”.

2025年是充滿挑戰與深刻變革的一年。中國                    2025 was a year of considerable challenges and profound
消費市場正經歷結構性轉型，由「規模擴張」向                    transformation. China’s consumer market is experiencing structural
「質量提升」加速演進。面對行業競爭及消費                     transformation, with accelerated evolution from “scale expansion”
者 行 為 更 趨 理 性 的 外 部 環 境，本 集 團 憑 藉 著      to “quality improvement”. Faced with an external environment
經 驗 豐 富 的 經 營 團 隊、穩 健 的 獲 利 模 式 與 風      shaped by industrial competition and increasingly rational consumer
險 控 管 機 制，結 合 持 續 優 化 的 組 織 協 同 管 理      behavior, the Group managed to maintain steady progress through
效能，成功保持穩步前行，實現收益與利潤創                     its experienced management team, robust profit-making model and
歷 史 新 高 的 豐 碩 果 實。這 份 來 之 不 易 的 成 績      risk management mechanism, combined with continuously optimised
單，是我們戰略定力、組織韌性與紮實基本功                     organisational synergy and management efficiency, achieving a fruitful
的集中體現。透過敏銳捕捉市場脈動，我們靈                     and record-breaking achievement in both revenue and profits. This
活 調 整 策 略 並 優 化 商 品 結 構，將 資 源 精 準 投      hard-earned achievement represented a testament to our strategic
入最具潛力的領域，在變局中守住基本盤，在                     determination, resilient organisation and solid foundation. Through
復 甦 中 搶 佔 先 機。自2020年 以 來，本 集 團 收         our acute discernment in market development, we adjusted our
益年複合增長率達6.9%，其中飲品年收益複                    strategies with agility and optimised our product portfolio, to precisely
合增長率達9.2%。                               deploy our resources to the most promising sectors, enabling us to
                                         safeguard fundamentals in times of change, and seizing first-mover
                                         opportunities during the recovery. Since 2020, the Group’s revenue
                                         has been growing at a compound annual growth rate of 6.9%, in
                                         which the compound annual growth rate of the beverages reached
                                         9.2%.

未來展望                                     PROSPECT

面 對 消 費 趨 於 理 性 且 多 元 的 結 構 性 變 革，本      Faced with a structural transformation towards more rational and
集團始終秉持長期主義與「三好一公道」理念，                    diverse consumption patterns, the Group has consistently adhered to
透 過 價 值 營 銷 與 數 位 化 賦 能，在 確 保 價 盤 穩      long-termism and the philosophy of “Three Goods and One Fairness
定 及 通 路 健 康 的 基 礎 上，持 續 深 化 市 場 滲 透      （三好一公道）”. Through value-based marketing strategies and
並構築品牌壁壘。展望2026年，隨經濟溫和復                   digital empowerment, the Group persisted in deepening market
甦與內需動能激活，我們將以「聚焦經營」為核                    penetration and built brand barriers on the basis of ensuring price
心，精準洞察健康與情緒價值等趨勢，藉由研                     stability and healthy distribution channels. Looking ahead to 2026,
發 創 新 全 面 提 升 商 品 力，在 迭 代 優 化 的 市 場      with modest economic recovery and domestic demand activation,
格局中追求「持續成功」與「永遠進步」            ，為股東       the Group will put emphasis on the core philosophy of “focused
創造長期穩健的價值回報。                             operation（聚焦經營）”, through precise insights into trends such as
                                         health and emotional value, comprehensively driving product strength
                                         enhancement by research and development innovation. Amidst an
                                         ever-evolving market, the Group strives for “sustained success（持續
                                         成功）” and “relentless progress（永遠進步）”, creating long-term and
                                         stable value to shareholders.

               統一企業中國控股有限公司 Uni-President China Holdings Ltd.    • Annual Report 2025 年報                             7
    管理層討論及分析
    Management Discussion & Analysis

    經濟環境分析                                   ANALYSIS ON ECONOMIC ENVIRONMENT

    2025年，中國消費市場在結構優化中穩步前                    In 2025, China’s consumer market continued to advance steadily
    行，國內生產總值(GDP)同比增長5.0%，社會                 amid structural optimization, with a year-on-year increase of 5.0% in
    消費品零售總額增長3.7%。在政策賦能與技                    gross domestic product (“GDP”) and a year-on-year growth of 3.7%
    術創新的驅動下，消費市場邁向「總量穩增、                     in total retail sales of consumer goods. Powered by policy support
    結構優化、業態革新、區域協同」的高質量發                     and technological innovation, the consumer market was moving
    展階段。消費支出作為經濟核心引擎，引領中                     toward a high-quality development stage characterized by “stable
    國 穩 居 全 球 第 二 大 消 費 市 場。面 對 日 益 活 躍      growth in aggregate volume, structural optimization, business model
    的市場競爭，本集團始終堅守價值導向，維持                     innovation, and regional synergy（總量穩增、結構優化、業態革新、
    價 格 體 系 穩 定，以 卓 越 的 商 品 力 與 價 值 創 新      區域協同）”. As the core engine of the economy, consumer spending
    深 化 消 費 者 聯 結，致 力 於 推 動 產 業 秩 序 的 良      has firmly secured China’s position as the world’s second-largest
    性發展。                                     consumer market. Confronted with increasingly vigorous market
                                             competition, the Group has steadfastly adhered to a value-oriented
                                             approach, maintained price stability, and deepened consumer
                                             connections through exceptional product strength and value
                                             innovation, striving to promote the healthy industry development.

    面對原物料市場價格與全球貿易格局的動態                      In response to the dynamic changes in raw material market prices
    演變，本集團展現了前瞻性的決策能力。我們                     and the global trade landscape, the Group has demonstrated
    持 續 深 化 宏 觀 形 勢 研 判，透 過 完 善 組 織 協 同      forward-looking decision-making capabilities. We continuously
    機制，系統性提升應對外部變化的韌性。藉由                     deepened our analysis on the macro situation, and systematically
    精 益 生 產、優 化 產 品 結 構 及 多 元 化 供 應 鏈 佈      enhanced our resilience in coping with external changes by
    局，我們有效調節成本變動帶來的影響，並始                     improving the organizational coordination mechanism. Through lean
    終 秉 持 效 益 精 進 原 則，實 現 資 源 配 置 的 最 優      production, optimized product structure and diversified supply chain
    化。                                       layout, we effectively mitigated the impact of cost fluctuations while
                                             consistently upholding the principle of improvement in efficiency to
                                             achieve optimal resource allocation.

    當 前 消 費 決 策 更 趨 成 熟 與 理 性，追 求 極 致 價      Currently, consumers have become more mature and rational
    值的同時，對產品品質、健康功能與情緒價值                     in decision-making. While pursuing ultimate value, consumers
    提 出 了 更 高 期 待。悅 己 經 濟 與 情 感 連 結 成 為      have higher expectations for product quality, health functions
    驅 動 增 長 的 關 鍵，消 費 者 愈 發 重 視 產 品 帶 來      and emotional value. The self-pleasure economy and emotional

 得 開 心」為 使 命，向 消 費 者 提 供 安 全、健 康、         Fair Price （品質好、信用好、服務好、價格公道）, with the mission
 美 味 的 食 品。本 集 團 成 立 以 總 經 理 為 主 任 的      of producing products that consumers will “be comfortable and
 食 安 品 質 委 員 會，作 為 食 品 安 全 和 品 質 保 證      happy to eat （吃得安心、吃得開心）”, we offer safe, healthy and

 覆蓋商業研發所、採購資源管理室、生產管理                     and Quality Committee with our General Manager as its Director.
 室 及 食 品 安 全 管 理 室 等 相 關 部 門，定 期 舉 行      The Committee serves as the organisation with supreme power to

 討。此外，本集團設立食品安全管理室為食品                     The members of the Food Safety and Quality Committee cover
 安 全 和 品 質 保 證 的 管 理 部 門，負 責 食 品 安 全      all relevant departments including the business research institute,
 和品質保證系統的佈建、政策的執行及落實，                     the procurement resources management office, the production

本集團建立食品安全三道防線，即資質審查、                     The Group has established three lines of defence for food
實地評鑒、食品安全檢測，通過多角度對供應                     safety, namely, qualification review, field assessment, and food
商 的 食 品 安 全 進 行 管 理，有 效 控 制 供 應 商 帶      safety testing. By managing suppliers’ food safety from multiple
入的食品安全風險，確保供應商食品安全。                      perspectives, we have effectively controlled the food safety risk
                                         brought by suppliers and ensured the food safety of suppliers.

本集團堅持品質三道把關：開發設計品質把                      The Group adheres to three controls to ensure quality and prevent
關、生產過程品質把關、出廠通路品質把關，                     abnormalities in quality: quality control on product development and
有效管理產品品質，預防品質異常的發生。本                     design, quality control on the production process, and quality control
集 團 產 品 除 嚴 格 遵 守《中 華 人 民 共 和 國 食 品      on finished products. The Group strictly abides by relevant laws and
安全法 》等法律法規的規定，還對所有產品的                    regulations such as the Food Safety Law of the People’s Republic of
食 品 安 全 項 目 嚴 格 執 行 監 控，確 保 產 品 符 合      China. Besides, the food safety of all products is strictly monitored
法律法規要求，讓消費者「吃得安心、吃得開                     to ensure they comply with the relevant laws and regulations and are
心」。                                      “comfortable and happy to eat（吃得安心、吃得開心）”.

本集團食品安全管理室下設食品安全檢測中                      The Group’s Food Safety and Management Office has set up the
心，為食品安全風險管控提供強有力的保障。                     Food Safety Testing Centre to provide strong support for food
自2005年起，該中心每年通過中國合格評定                    safety risk control. Since 2005, the Centre has passed the annual
國家認可委員會專家評審(CNAS國家認可），                   China National Accreditation Service for Conformity Assessment
至今已通過173項檢測項目；同時擁有多項實                    (CNAS accreditation). To date, 173 projects of the Group have been

項；食 品 安 全 項 目 的 檢 測 能 力 覆 蓋 飲 料、食        for utility models and more than 700 perennial independent testing
品、水、添加劑、糧油與包裝材料等品類。                      projects. The scope of assessment on the Group’s food safety

 截 至2025年12月31日 止 年 度（「本 年 度」），            For the year ended 31 December 2025 (the “Year”), the Group
 本 集 團 錄 得 收 益 人 民 幣31,714.3百 萬 元，較        recorded revenue of RMB31,714.3 million, representing an increase
 2024年 之 收 益 人 民 幣30,331.5百 萬 元 上 升        of 4.6% from RMB30,331.5 million for 2024. Revenue from the

事指導的政策與程序制訂財務風險管理政                       channels. The Group’s finance department has formulated financial

 度，本 集 團 於 財 務 風 險 管 理 方 面 繼 續 維 持 審      The Group continued to adopt a conservative approach to financial
 慎態度，並無重大銀行借貸。由於本集團大部                     risk management with no significant bank borrowing during the Year.
 分 收 入 源 自 中 國 的 業 務，故 大 部 分 資 產 收 款      Most of the Group’s receipts and payments were denominated in
 及 付 款 貨 幣 均 以 人 民 幣 計 值。本 集 團 若 因 以      Renminbi since a majority of its revenue was derived from operations

 及借款）而承受外匯風險時，將適時運用外匯                     when appropriate, for risk aversion when it is exposed to foreign

                                         POLICY

於2025年12月31日，本 集 團 僱 員 總 人 數 為           As at 31 December 2025, the total number of employees of the
34,305人。本集團秉持聚焦經營、簡單操作的                  Group was 34,305. The Group adhered to the policies of focused
原則，持續優化組織和人力結構，精進企業體                     and streamlined operation, and organisational structure and staff
質，提升效率。在用人策略上，本集團秉持精                     structure were improved on a continuous basis to build a stronger
兵簡政、用人唯才的人資策略，堅持完善的培                     and efficient enterprise. In terms of recruitment, the Group was
訓、人文關懷等機制，確保團隊穩定及戰力提                     streamlining its administration and progressing towards meritocracy.
升。透過內部進階培訓、有計劃的崗位輪調、                     The Group insisted on building a stable and robust team through
外部交流學習等，不斷提升綜合能力，為集團                     mechanisms including comprehensive training and humane care.
核 心 崗 位 需 求 提 供 保 障，同 時，通 過 內 部 拔        Internal training, regular position transfer, external exchange
擢為主、外部聘用為輔、績效考核等機制，確                     and part-time study and other measures enhanced our overall
保 現 有 管 理 團 隊 的 活 力 與 戰 力。為 實 現 年 度      performance and secured talents for key positions of the Group.
制 定 的 各 項 目 標，本 集 團 設 立 績 效 獎 金 及 獎      Meanwhile, priorities were given to internal promotion over external
勵 計 劃，嘉 許 及 鼓 勵 於 本 集 團 業 務 作 出 傑 出      recruitment, and performance appraisal and other measures were
貢 獻 的 各 級 僱 員。績 效 獎 金 根 據 本 集 團 整 體      implemented to keep the current management team ambitious
以 及 各 業 務 單 位 達 成 之 收 益、利 潤 等 目 標 以      and strong. To achieve the Group’s annual goals, the Group had
及 僱 員 的 績 效 評 核 發 放。本 集 團 酬 金 政 策 依      performance bonuses and incentive schemes in place to commend

市 場 可 比 資 料 及 本 集 團 的 表 現 作 出 回 報。作      contributions to the Group’s business. Performance bonuses
為 本 集 團 酬 金 政 策 的 一 環，本 集 團 與 其 僱 員      were distributed on the basis of the realized earnings and profits
訂立個別僱傭合約，當中涉及工資、社會保障                     objectives of individual business units and the Group as a whole, as

保 密 責 任 以 及 終 止 條 件 等。本 年 度，總 員 工        Group’s remuneration policy rewarded our employees and directors

層將取得充分市場資訊以確定相關當前市                       CCT Agreements, the management of the Company would obtain

立第三方資料。例如，管理層將審閱並以商業                     market rate, payment terms and practices, as well as the latest
角度評估來自至少兩名獨立供應商或服務供                      market and independent third party information. For example, the
應 商 之 報 價，以 確 保 統 一 企 業 集 團 將 採 購 之      management would review quotations from at least two independent
產品或服務價格與同一期間由獨立第三方所                      suppliers or service providers and evaluate them from a commercial
提 供 之 該 等 產 品 或 服 務 相 若。倘 並 無 足 夠 可      perspective, so as to ensure that the products or services to be
資 比 較 交 易，該 等 產 品 或 服 務 之 價 格 將 參 照      procured from the UPE Group are of comparable prices for such
過 往 價 格 及 成 本 分 析 按 公 平 基 準 釐 定。同 樣      products or services being offered by the independent third parties
地，管 理 層 亦 透 過 審 閱 市 場 資 訊 以 釐 定 擬 向      of the same period. Where there are no sufficient comparable
統 一 企 業 集 團 提 供 之 產 品 或 服 務 價 格，以 確      transactions available, the prices of such products or services would
保該等價格與本集團向獨立第三方提供之產                      be determined on arm’s length basis with reference to historical
品或服務價格相若。                                prices and cost analysis. Likewise, the management would also

                                         are of comparable prices for such products or services being offered
                                         to independent third parties by the Group.

日 止 年 度 本 集 團 業 務、財 務 關 鍵 績 效 指 標、        The discussion on a fair review of the Group’s business, financial
業績及預計日後發展的公平回顧討論載於本                       key performance indicators and performance and indication of future

主要風險及不明朗因素                                Principal risks and uncertainties

多項因素可能影響本集團業績及業務營運，                       There are a number of factors which may affect the results and
其中若干因素為飲料及方便麵業務固有風                        business operations of the Group, some of which are inherent to
險，其餘則來源自外部因素。主要風險概述如                      beverages and instant noodles business and some are from external
下。                                        sources. Major risks are summarized below.

(i)   消費者偏好風險                             (i)   Consumer preferences risk

      本集團的成功依賴於其有能力預測消費                         The Group’s success depends on its ability to anticipate the
      者口味及飲食習慣並提供符合其偏好的                         tastes and dietary habits of consumers and to offer products
      產品。若本公司無法對消費者的偏好變                         that appeal to their preferences. The Company’s failure to
      化 作 出 預 測、識 別 或 反 應，則 可 能 導 致             anticipate, identify or react to the changes in consumer
      本集團產品的需求下降，從而導致庫存                         preferences could result in reduced demand for the Group’s
      過剩和銷量減少。本集團將在季度內繼                         products, which would, in turn, lead to excessive inventory

 董事會的職位及責任                                Roles and Responsibilities of the Board

 董 事 會 負 責 領 導 及 掌 控 本 公 司，監 督 本 集 團      The Board is responsible for the leadership and control of the
 業務、投資及戰略決策，維持本集團風險管理                     Company and oversees the Group’s businesses, investment and
 及 內 部 控 制 系 統 的 有 效 性，以 及 監 督 本 集 團      strategic decisions, maintaining the effectiveness of the Group’s
 的表現。董事會目前下設四個委員會，即審核                     risk management and internal control systems, and monitoring the
 委員會（「審核委員會」）、提名委員會（「提名                   performance of the Group. There are currently four committees
 委員會」）、薪酬委員會（「薪酬委員會」）及投                   established under the Board, being the audit committee (the
 資、戰略及發展委員會（       「投資、戰略及發展委             “Audit Committee”), the nomination committee (the “Nomination
 員會」 ）。各委員會均有其職權範圍，並定期向                   Committee”), the remuneration committee (the “Remuneration
 董事會報告。                                   Committee”) and the investment, strategy and development
                                          committee (the “Investment, Strategy and Development Committee”).
                                          Each committee has its terms of reference and reports to the Board
                                          regularly.

 本 公 司 並 無 行 政 總 裁，其 職 責 由 本 集 團 總 經      The Company does not have a chief executive officer, whose role is
 理（「總經理」）劉新華先生履行。本公司主席                    instead performed by the president of the Group (the “President”),
 （「主席」）及總經理的職位由不同人士擔任。                    Mr. Liu Xinhua. The roles of the chairman of the Company (the
 主席羅智先先生負責確保各董事妥善履行彼                      “Chairman”) and the President are performed by separate persons.
 等職責，並確保及時就重大事項進行討論。日                     The Chairman, Mr. Lo Chih-Hsien, is responsible for ensuring that
 常營運及執行董事會所設定的策略及方針的                      the Directors perform their duties properly and ensuring discussions
 權力及授權乃授予本公司由總經理領導的管                      on material matters take place on a timely basis. The power and
 理團隊（「管理層」）。管理層對本集團的營運                    authority to carry out day-to-day operations and implementation of

 企業管治職能                                   CORPORATE GOVERNANCE FUNCTIONS

 董事會並無成立企業管治委員會。                          The Board has not established a corporate governance committee.

 反而，整個董事會負責履行企業管治職能，包                     Instead, the full Board is responsible for performing the corporate
 括(i)制定及審閱本公司企業管治之政策及常                    governance function including (i) developing and reviewing the
 規；(ii)審閱及監控本公司的董事及高級管理層                  Company’s policies and practices on corporate governance, (ii)
 培訓及持續專業發展；(iii)審閱及監控本公司                  reviewing and monitoring training and continuous professional
 有關遵守適用的法定及監管規定之政策及常                      development of Directors and senior management of the Company,
 規及(iv)制定、審閱及監察適用於本集團僱員                   (iii) reviewing and monitoring the Company’s policies and practices
 及董事的操守準則及合規手冊。本年度，董事                     on compliance with the applicable legal and regulatory requirements
 會已審閱本公司企業管治守則的遵守情況及                      and (iv) developing, reviewing and monitoring the code of conduct

                                          the Directors. During the Year, the Board reviewed the Company’s

                                          in its Corporate Governance Report.

風險管理及內部監控                                RISK MANAGEMENT AND INTERNAL
                                         CONTROL

董事會整體負責維持本集團穩健而有效之風                      The Board has overall responsibilities for maintaining a sound and
險 管 理 及 內 部 監 控 系 統。該 系 統 包 含 一 個 清      effective risk management and internal control system of the Group.
晰 明 確 界 定 權 限 之 管 理 架 構，旨 在 協 助 本 集      The system includes a defined management structure with limits of
團 識 別 及 管 理 重 大 風 險，以 達 成 本 集 團 的 整      authority, and is designed to help the Group to identify and manage
體業務目標，釐定本集團的風險狀況，監督風                     significant risks to achieve the Group’s overall business objectives,
險管理框架結構，審核主要風險及緩解戰略，                     determine the Group’s risk profile, oversee risk management
以及保障風險管理的成效及遵守相關法律法                      framework structure, review key risks and mitigation strategies and to
規。                                       ensure risk management effectiveness and compliance with relevant
                                         laws and regulations.

本 集 團 利 用 自 下 而 上 及 自 上 而 下 的 方 式，確      A bottom-up and top-down approach is utilised to ensure a holistic
保 擁 有 一 套 完 善 的 風 險 管 理 程 序。自 下 而 上      risk management process. The bottom-up approach is supported
的方式以核心業務單位識別風險及確定風險                      by key business units to identify and prioritise risks while the top-
評級為基礎；而自上而下的方式則審閱及評                      down approach reviews and assesses if risks are comprehensively
估核心業務單位於實現本集團的目標時是否                      identified, prioritised and properly addressed by key business units
已 全 面 識 別 風 險、為 風 險 劃 分 級 別 及 予 以 妥      to accomplish the Group’s objectives. The management team
善處理。管理層團隊定期監測新出現的風險，                     regularly monitors emerging risks for continuous risk assessment
以 進 行 持 續 風 險 評 估，建 立 以 風 險 管 理 為 基      purpose and for building the risk-management based internal audit
礎 的 內 部 審 核 計 劃，並 週 期 性 評 估 風 險 管 理      plan and periodically assesses the adequacy and effectiveness of
及 內 部 控 制 的 適 當 性 及 有 效 性，以 保 證 持 續      risk management and internal controls for ongoing risk assurance
的 風 險 擔 保。此 舉 提 高 本 集 團 風 險 管 理 及 控      purposes. This enhances the effectiveness of the Group’s risk
制框架的有效性。                                 management and control framework.

部消息可妥善處理及發佈。於本年度，倘事件                       inside information to ensure that inside information is handled and
對本公司股份之價格或交易量構成影響時，                        disseminated properly. During the Year, the Company regarded

 股東權利                                    SHAREHOLDERS’ RIGHTS

 (1)   股東召開股東特別大會之程序                     (1)   Procedures for shareholders to convene an EGM

       根據組織章程，股東特別大會可應本公                       According to the Articles of Association, an EGM may be
       司任何一名或多名股東的書面請求而召                       convened on the written requisition of any one or more
       開，而該等股東於遞呈請求當日合共持                       members of the Company holding together, as at the date of
       有不少於本公司繳足股本十分之一（附                       deposit of the requisition, shares representing not less than
       有權利可於本公司股東大會上投票）的                       one-tenth of the paid up capital of the Company which carry
       股份。書面請求須存放於本公司主要辦                       the right of voting at general meetings of the Company. The
       事處，列明會議目的及有待加入會議議                       written requisition shall be deposited at the principal office of
       程之決議案，並由請求人士簽署。                         the Company, specifying the objects of the meeting and the
                                               resolutions to be added to the meeting agenda, and signed by
                                               the requisitionist(s).

       倘於遞呈請求之日起計21天內，董事會                      If within twenty-one days of such deposit, the Board fails to
       並無著手召開股東特別大會，則請求人                       proceed to convene the EGM, the requisitionist(s) or any of
       士或佔彼等全部投票權一半以上的任何                       them representing more than one-half of the total voting rights
       遞呈請求人士可自行召開股東特別大                        of all of them, may themselves convene an EGM, but any
       會，惟按上述方式召開的任何大會不得                       meeting so convened shall not be held after the expiration of
       於遞呈有關請求之日起計三個月屆滿後                       three months from the date of the deposit of the requisition
       舉行，且本公司應向遞呈請求人士償付                       and all reasonable expenses incurred by the requisitionist(s)

 任是閱讀其他信息，在此過程中，考慮其他信                      our responsibility is to read the other information and, in doing so,

 在 擬 備 綜 合 財 務 報 表 時，董 事 負 責 評 估 貴 集       In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors are
 團 持 續 經 營 的 能 力，並 在 適 用 情 況 下 披 露 與       responsible for assessing the Group’s ability to continue as a going

 會 計 基 礎，除 非 董 事 有 意 將 貴 集 團 清 盤 或 停       and using the going concern basis of accounting unless the directors
 止經營，或別無其他實際的替代方案。                         either intend to liquidate the Group or to cease operations, or have

核數師就審計綜合財務報表承擔                           AUDITOR’S RESPONSIBILITIES FOR THE
的責任                                      AUDIT OF THE CONSOLIDATED FINANCIAL
                                         STATEMENTS

我 們 的 目 標，是 對 綜 合 財 務 報 表 整 體 是 否 不      Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether
存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述                      the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from
取 得 合 理 保 證，並 出 具 包 括 我 們 意 見 的 核 數      material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to issue
師報告。我們僅向 閣下（作為整體）報告，除                    an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. We report our opinion
此 之 外 本 報 告 別 無 其 他 目 的。我 們 不 會 就 本      solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do not
報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承擔任何                      assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other person
責任。合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證                     for the contents of this report. Reasonable assurance is a high level
按 照 香 港 審 計 準 則 進 行 的 審 計，在 某 一 重 大      of assurance, but is not a guarantee that an audit conducted in
錯 誤 陳 述 存 在 時 總 能 發 現。錯 誤 陳 述 可 以 由      accordance with HKSAs will always detect a material misstatement
欺 詐 或 錯 誤 引 起，如 果 合 理 預 期 它 們 單 獨 或      when it exists. Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are
匯總起來可能影響綜合財務報表使用者依賴                      considered material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could
綜 合 財 務 報 表 所 作 出 的 經 濟 決 定，則 有 關 的      reasonably be expected to influence the economic decisions of users
錯誤陳述可被視作重大。                              taken on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.

在 根 據 香 港 審 計 準 則 進 行 審 計 的 過 程 中，我      As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise
們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態度。我                     professional judgment and maintain professional scepticism
們亦：                                      throughout the audit. We also:

•   識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致綜                     •    Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement of the
    合財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風險，                         consolidated financial statements, whether due to fraud or
    設計及執行審計程序以應對這些風險，                         error, design and perform audit procedures responsive to
    以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑證，作為                         those risks, and obtain audit evidence that is sufficient and
    我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐可能涉及串                         appropriate to provide a basis for our opinion. The risk of not
    謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛假陳述，或凌駕                        detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is higher
    於內部控制之上，因此未能發現因欺詐                         than for one resulting from error, as fraud may involve collusion,
    而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風險高於未能                         forgery, intentional omissions, misrepresentations, or the
    發現因錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述的風                         override of internal control.
    險。

      2.2 附屬公司                                   2.2 Subsidiaries

          2.2.1 綜合                                    2.2.1 Consolidation

          附屬公司指本集團具有控制權之                               A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity)
          實體（包括結構實體）      。本集團就參                       over which the Group has control. The Group controls an
          與該實體之可變回報承受風險或                               entity where the Group is exposed to, or has rights to,
          擁 有 權 利，並 有 能 力 通 過 對 該 實                    variable returns from its involvement with the entity and
          體 之 權 力 影 響 該 等 回 報 時，即 本                    has the ability to affect those returns through its power
          集 團 控 制 對 該 實 體。附 屬 公 司 由                    over the entity. Subsidiaries are consolidated from the
          控制權轉移至本集團當日開始綜                               date on which control is transferred to the Group. They
          合 入 賬，並 由 失 去 控 制 權 當 日 開                    are deconsolidated from the date that control ceases.
          始不再綜合入賬。

          集 團 內 公 司 間 之 交 易、結 餘 及 交                    Inter-company transactions, balances and unrealised
          易 之 未 變 現 利 得 均 予 以 對 銷。未                    gains on transactions between group companies are
          變 現 虧 損 亦 予 以 對 銷。附 屬 公 司                    eliminated. Unrealised losses are also eliminated. When
          所 匯 報 金 額 已 按 需 要 作 出 調 整，                   necessary, amounts reported by subsidiaries have
          以確保與本集團之會計政策保持                               been adjusted to conform with the Group’s accounting

      任而非合營安排之法律結構。                               than the legal structure of the joint arrangement.

      分－或兩者）   ，而並非：(a)用於生產                      (a) use in the production of supply of goods or services

      投 資 物 業 初 步 按 成 本 確 認，其 後                  Investment properties are initially recognised at cost

      損列賬。折舊以直線法計算，於40                           depreciation and accumulated impairment losses.
      年的估計可使用年期內將可折舊                             Depreciation is calculated using a straight-line method

      使 用 年 期 於 各 結 算 日 檢 討，並 在                  useful lives of 40 years. The residual values and useful
      適當情況下作出調整。                                 lives of investment properties are reviewed, and adjusted

       設立貸款融資時支付的費用倘部                              Fees paid on the establishment of loan facilities are
       分或全部融資將會很有可能提                               recognised as transaction costs of the loan to the extent
       取，該 費 用 可 遞 延 確 認 為 交 易 成                   that it is probable that some or all of the facility will be

       並按有關融資期間攤銷。                                 liquidity services and amortised over the period of the

          與 收 購、建 築 或 生 產 合 資 格 資 產                   General and specific borrowing costs directly attributable

          其擬定用途或出售為止。                                 such time as the assets are substantially ready for their
                                                      intended use or sale.

          合資格資產未獲撥付開支的特定                              Investment income earned on the temporary investment

          除。                                          eligible for capitalisation.

          所有其他借貸成本於產生期間於                              All other borrowing costs are recognised in the income
          損益表中確認。                                     statement in the period in which they are incurred.

    2.16 撥備                                  2.16 Provisions

       倘本集團需就過去事項承擔現有                              Provisions are recognised when the Group has a present
       法 律 或 推 定 責 任，而 有 可 能 導 致                   legal or constructive obligation as a result of past events;
       資 源 流 出 以 履 行 該 責 任，並 能 可                   it is probable that an outflow of resources will be required
       靠 估 計 金 額 才 會 確 認 撥 備。並 無                   to settle the obligation; and the amount has been reliably
       就未來經營虧損確認撥備。                                estimated. Provisions are not recognised for future
                                                   operating losses.

       倘 出 現 多 項 類 似 債 務，會 否 導 致                   Where there are a number of similar obligations, the
       經濟利益流出以清償債務乃經考                              likelihood that an outflow will be required in settlement is
       慮 債 務 之 整 體 類 別 後 確 定。即 使                   determined by considering the class of obligations as a
       同類別債務中任何一項可能流出                              whole. A provision is recognised even if the likelihood of
       經 濟 利 益 之 機 會 不 大，仍 會 確 認                   an outflow with respect to any one item included in the
       撥備。                                         same class of obligations may be small.

       撥備乃按管理層對清償債項所需                              Provisions are measured at the present value of
       開 支 之 最 佳 估 計 之 現 值，使 用 反                   management’s best estimate of the expenditure required

       值，以 及 該 項 負 債 特 定 之 風 險 之                   current market assessments of the time value of money
       除 稅 前 稅 率 計 算。因 時 間 的 流 逝                   and the risks specific to the liability. The increase in the
       導致的撥備增加則確認為利息開                              provision due to the passage of time is recognised as
       支。                                          interest expense.

      2.17 收益確認（續）                              2.17 Revenue recognition (Continued)

          產品通常以一段時期內的銷售總                              The products are often sold with retrospective volume
          量 為 基 準，進 行 追 溯 批 量 折 扣 銷                   discounts based on aggregate sales over a period of
          售。該 等 銷 售 的 收 益 乃 基 於 合 約                   time. Revenue from these sales is recognised based on
          規 定 的 價 格，經 扣 除 估 計 批 量 折                   the price specified in the contract, net of the estimated
          扣 後 確 認。本 公 司 使 用 累 積 的 經                   volume discounts. Accumulated experience is used to
          驗 估 計 及 提 供 折 扣，且 收 益 僅 於                   estimate and provide for the discounts, and revenue is
          重大撥回極大可能不會產生時確                              only recognised to the extent that it is highly probable
          認。當 預 期 向 客 戶 應 付 有 關 銷 售                   that a significant reversal will not occur. A refund liability
          的批量折扣時確認退款責任（包括                             (included in other payables and accruals) is recognised
          在其他應付賬款及應計費用內）。                             for expected volume discounts payable to customers in
          由 於 銷 售 之 信 貸 期 為60至90日，                    relation to sales. No significant element of financing is
          符 合 市 場 慣 例，故 並 不 存 在 重 大                   deemed present as the sales are made with a credit term
          融資因素。                                       of 60-90 days, which is consistent with market practice.

          本集團在貨品交付時確認應收款                              A receivable is recognised when the goods are delivered
          項，由於此時代價屬無條件，故本                             as this is the point in time that the consideration is
          集團僅需於付款逾期前等待客戶                              unconditional because only the passage of time is
          付款。                                         required before the payment is due.

          本集團有責任向質保期內的瑕疵                              The Group’s obligations to provide a refund for faulty
          產 品 提 供 退 款。本 集 團 於 銷 售 時                   products are under the standard warranty terms.
          使 用 累 積 經 驗 估 計 有 關 退 款。因                   Accumulated experience is used to estimate such returns
          產 品 規 模 大 及 單 個 產 品 價 值 低，                  at the time of sale. Because of the large size and low
          故 退 貨 量 並 不 重 大。已 確 認 累 積                   value of each individual product, the amount of products
          收益之重大撥回極大可能不會產                              returned were immaterial. It is highly probable that a
          生。因此，概無就退貨確認退款負                             significant reversal in the cumulative revenue recognised
          債。本 集 團 於 各 報 告 日 期 重 新 評                   will not occur. Therefore, no refund liability for goods
          估上述假設之有效性及對退款金                              return was recognized. The validity of this assumption
          額的估計。                                       and the estimated amount of returns are reassessed at
                                                      each reporting date.

          對於向客戶支付促銷活動的若干                              For certain payments to customers for promotion
          款 項，本 集 團 未 向 客 戶 提 供 明 確                   activities, the Group did not provide a distinct good
          的 商 品 或 服 務，因 此 記 錄 為 銷 售                   or service to customers and therefore recorded as a
          價格的扣減。                                      deduction of sales price.

          本集團預計不會有任何包含融資                              The Group does not expect to have any contracts
          成分的合約。因此，本集團並未調                             containing financing components. As a consequence, the
          整貨幣時間價值的任何過渡價格。                             Group does not adjust any of the transition prices for the
                                                      time value of money.

    2.18 政府補助                                2.18 Government grants

       補助及本集團將符合所有附帶條                              where there is a reasonable assurance that the grant will

    2.19 租賃                                  2.19 Leases

       租 賃 確 認 為 使 用 權 資 產，並 在 租                   Leases are recognised as a right-of-use asset and a
       賃資產可供本集團使用之日確認                              corresponding liability at the date at which the leased
       相應負債。                                       asset is available for use by the Group.

       租賃產生的資產及負債初步以現                              Assets and liabilities arising from a lease are initially
       值 基 準 計 量。租 賃 負 債 包 括 固 定                   measured on a present value basis. Lease liabilities
       付款（包括實質固定付款）的現值                             include the net present value of fixed payments (including
       淨額減任何應收租賃優惠。                                in-substance fixed payments), less any lease incentives
                                                   receivable.

       根據合理確定擴大選擇權作出的                              Lease payments to be made under reasonably certain
       租賃付款亦計入負債的計量。                               extension options are also included in the measurement
                                                   of the liability.

       租賃付款採用租賃所隱含的利率                              The lease payments are discounted using the interest
       予以貼現。倘無法釐定該利率（本                             rate implicit in the lease. If that rate cannot be readily
       集團的租賃一般屬此類情況），則                             determined, which is generally the case for leases in the
       使 用 承 租 人 增 量 借 款 利 率，即 個                   Group, the lessee’s incremental borrowing rate is used,
       別承租人在類似經濟環境中按類                              being the rate that the individual lessee would have to
       似 條 款、抵 押 及 條 件 借 入 獲 得 與                   pay to borrow the funds necessary to obtain an asset
       使用權資產價值類似的資產所需                              of similar value to the right-of-use asset in a similar
       資金必須支付的利率。                                  economic environment with similar terms, security and
                                                   conditions.

       後有任何增加時，即確認利得，惟                            increases in fair value less costs to sell of an asset,

                項

          應付貿易賬款及其他應付款項為                                 Trade and other payables are obligations to pay for
          在日常業務過程中向供應商購買                                 goods or services that have been acquired in the ordinary
          商 品 或 服 務 而 應 支 付 之 義 務。如                      course of business from suppliers. Trade and other
          應付貿易賬款及其他應付款項支                                 payable are classified as current liabilities if payment is

          業 務 經 營 週 期 中，則 可 較 長 時 間）                     cycle of the business if longer). If not, they are presented
          將 被 分 類 為 流 動 負 債；否 則 按 非                      as non-current liabilities.
          流動負債呈列。

          應付貿易賬款及其他應付款項初                                 Trade and other payables are recognised initially at fair
          步 以 公 平 值 確 認，其 後 採 用 實 際                      value and subsequently measured at amortised cost
          利息法按攤銷成本計量。                                    using the effective interest method.

      本集團管理資本之目的為：                                 The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to:

      •     保障本集團繼續以持續經營                           •     safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going
            方 式 經 營 的 能 力，務 求 繼 續                        concern, so that they can continue to provide
            為股東提供回報以及為其他                                 returns for shareholders and benefits for other
            權益持有人帶來利益；及                                  stakeholders, and

      •     維持最佳資本結構以減少資                           •     maintain an optimal capital structure to reduce the
            金成本。                                         cost of capital.

      為 維 持 或 調 整 資 本 結 構，本 集 團                    In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the
      可 調 整 向 股 東 支 付 股 息 之 金 額、                   Group may adjust the amount of dividends paid to
      發行新股份或出售資產以減少債                               shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to reduce
      項。                                           debt.

      與 行 業 其 他 公 司 一 樣，本 集 團 根                    Consistent with others in the industry, the Group
      據 資 本 負 債 比 率 監 控 其 資 本。資                    monitors capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This
      本負債比率乃按現金淨額除以總                               ratio is calculated as net cash divided by total equity.
      權益計算。

             本集團的若干物業及設備租賃包                                                 Extension and termination options are included in a

             最 大 化。所 持 有 的 大 部 分 延 期 及                                      flexibility in terms of managing contracts. The majority of

  28 其他儲備（續）                                28 OTHER RESERVES (CONTINUED)

      (b)   法定儲備                                 (b)   Statutory reserves

            中國法律與法規要求在中國註冊                             The PRC laws and regulations require companies
            的 公 司，在 對 權 益 持 有 人 作 溢 利                  registered in the PRC to provide for certain statutory
            分派前從各自的法定財務報表所                             reserves, which are appropriated from the net profit as
            申報的溢利淨額（在抵銷以往年度                            reported in their respective statutory financial statements
            的累計虧損後）中提撥一定的法定                            after offsetting accumulated losses from prior years and
            儲 備。所 有 法 定 儲 備 乃 就 特 定 目                  before profit distributions to equity holders. All statutory
            的而增設。                                      reserves are created for specific purposes.

            作為外商獨資企業和內資企業註                             PRC subsidiaries incorporated as wholly-foreign owned
            冊 成 立 的 中 國 附 屬 公 司，在 進 行                  enterprises and domestic companies are required to
            當 年 度 除 稅 後 溢 利 分 派 時，須 將                  appropriate 10% of statutory net profits to statutory
            法 定 溢 利 淨 額 之10%撥 作 法 定 盈                  surplus reserves, upon distribution of their post-tax
            餘 公 積 金，直 至 法 定 盈 餘 公 積 金                  profits of the current year, until the statutory surplus
            不少於公司之註冊資本50%為止。                           reserve is not less than 50% of its registered capital.
            作為中外合資經營企業註冊成立                             PRC subsidiaries incorporated as sino-foreign equity
            的 中 國 附 屬 公 司，在 其 各 自 董 事                  joint venture companies may allocate a portion of their
            會酌情決定下將部分法定除稅後                             statutory post-tax profits to the statutory reserves at the
            溢利撥入法定儲備。                                  discretion of the respective boards of directors.

            法定盈餘公積金只能用於彌補公                             The statutory surplus reserves shall only be used to make
            司 的 虧 損、擴 大 公 司 的 生 產 經 營                  up losses of the companies, to expand the companies’
            或增加公司資本。                                   production operations, or to increase the capital of the
                                                       companies.
